Two radios, one sky, one instant. There is still no injection here: d is inferred from a
coincidence model, not measured against a known input. The model's own consistency check
is the spread of f across the eight algorithms — but a check is only worth something if it
CAN fail, so every run rebuilds two joins where the model is definitionally false and prints
their spread beside the real one. Read the controls before the estimate.

HEADLINE — f-AGREEMENT CHECK: VACUOUS — THE NEGATIVE CONTROLS AGREE JUST AS CLOSELY
  The check passes on joins where the coincidence model is definitionally false (scrambled,
  shifted +2), so it is not testing the model. It CANNOT be read as evidence that the
  independence assumption holds.
  scrambled, shifted +2 agree MORE closely than the real pairing does.
  UNRESOLVED, and separately: f moves along 5 other axes (arm, channel, receiver pair, skew vs
      design bound, geometry) — further than across algorithms on arm, channel, receiver pair,
      skew vs design bound; ordered the same way by every algorithm on geometry. f belongs to
      the sky and must not move along any of them. Nothing in this report resolves that.

pairs 1167 (same-edge 558, opposite-edge 609; matched arms 1054)   cells 18672 (same-edge 8928, opposite-edge 9744)
receiver pairs lnb-c|lnb-b n=9336, lnb-d|lnb-b n=9336   arms 12
excluded lnb-a (14000 observations): flat ~1.19 at every tuning since 04:44 UTC: no signal path. Out of target AND null, because a dead port's null is silence and would pull every threshold down with it
skew median 0.0440 ms, max 8.3409 ms over 9336 tunings  <-- measured at barrier release, a LOWER BOUND on the true sample-start offset
  <-- 2702 of 9336 tunings, in 973 sweep(s), exceed the 0.054 ms the synchronised path was specified at. 'Same instant' is the
      claim everything below rests on, and on those tunings it is weaker than the design says.

OCCUPANCY f — one sky parameter, eight algorithms, and they must agree
  estimated on 16864 matched-arm cells (1808 unmatched-arm cells joined but left out: the model carries one p)
  discipline: cross-hardware. The same method decides on both radios, which share only the sky —
  not leave-one-out, because the model needs one statistic on both sides. See ROC below for that.
  this pools both geometries, and they do not mean quite the same thing: on a same-edge cell the
  two chains observe ONE tuning, while on an opposite-edge cell a coincidence means both edges of
  the channel were live at once. Per-geometry values are below; they are what to read if the two
  disagree.
  f spans 0.335 to 0.376 across 8 algorithms: spread 0.041, ratio 1.12x
  resampling the same cells 200x puts the spread at 0.038..0.048 (median 0.043) from sampling alone. The eight
  are resampled together, so the scale keeps the correlation of scoring one set of cells. It is
  optimistic about disagreement: max-minus-min cannot go below zero and its two extremes were
  picked out of the same cells. A scale to read the spread against, not a test.

  NEGATIVE CONTROLS — the same sweeps, the same thresholds, the same p, joined so that the
  coincidence model is DEFINITIONALLY FALSE. A tight f spread on the real join is evidence only if
  these come apart from it. Rebuilt every run; there is no flag that turns them off.
    join             cells         f range   spread  resample p05..p95   separates?
    real             16864    0.335..0.376    0.041       0.038..0.048   --
    scrambled        16864    0.705..0.744    0.039       0.030..0.056   NO  (TIGHTER than the real join)
    shifted +2       12648    0.701..0.740    0.039       0.032..0.060   NO  (TIGHTER than the real join)
      scrambled: radio A of one sweep + radio B of a DIFFERENT sweep — matched on arm and
      geometry; only the pairing is broken. 1054 sweep(s) joined.
      shifted +2: same sweep, same two radios, radio B moved 2 instants — matched on sweep,
      radios, arm, calibration; only the instant is wrong. 1054 sweep(s) joined.
      scrambled separation basis: resampled p05 0.030 against the real join's p95 0.048
      shifted +2 separation basis: resampled p05 0.032 against the real join's p95 0.048
  >>> VERDICT: VACUOUS — THE NEGATIVE CONTROLS AGREE JUST AS CLOSELY
      scrambled, shifted +2 destroy the simultaneity the model is built on, and the 8
      algorithms agree on f just as closely there as here. The agreement above is therefore a
      property of the algorithms, not of the sky: they correlate with one another at phi
      0.82-0.94 over these observations, so they are near-duplicates rather than 8 independent
      witnesses and their f values have to agree whatever they are fed.
      NOTHING BELOW IS VALIDATED BY THE SPREAD ABOVE. d stays what it was: a model output,
      with no injection behind it and now no working check either.
      rule: every negative control's resampled f spread must sit clear above the real join's
      before a tight real spread may be read as evidence of anything

  WHERE f ACTUALLY MOVES — f is a property of the sky, so it must be constant along EVERY axis.
  The algorithm axis checked above is the one with the LEAST variance available: eight scorers
  reading identical samples, correlated with each other at phi 0.82-0.94.

      'same sign' counts the algorithms that order the two extreme levels the same way. A gap
      SMALLER than the across-algorithm spread is not thereby noise: one that every algorithm
      orders the same way is systematic, and it is exactly the pattern the reviewers pinned on
      the receiver pair — mean +0.072, 8 of 8 algorithms, 0 of 300 cluster-bootstrap draws at
      or below zero.
      NOTHING IN THIS REPORT RESOLVES ANY OF THIS MOVEMENT.
      levels shows counted/found: a level under 24 cells, or one that fewer than 2 algorithms
      can solve, is found and listed but left out of the gap — the first swings by tenths on
      one extra coincidence and the second has no across-algorithm reading to compare against
      the axis above. A gap is only formed where two levels survive both cuts.

BY GEOMETRY — same-edge is replication; opposite-edge is both edges of one channel at one instant
   opposite-edge  cells 8864  f 0.344..0.388 over 8 algorithms   phi 0.502..0.573
       same-edge  cells 8000  f 0.323..0.366 over 8 algorithms   phi 0.513..0.582
  The 515 ms question: the two edges of one channel used to be scanned two dwells apart and
  their detection correlation capped at phi 0.73 while cross-method agreement on identical
  samples reached 0.97. Both were measured INSIDE one radio and are not directly comparable to
  anything here: a shared Pluto, ADC clock and USB bus inflate agreement, which is
  the whole reason this module crossed the hardware boundary. The like-for-like comparison is the
  two rows above — same hardware boundary, same instant, differing only in geometry.
  Simultaneous cross-radio phi averages 0.530 across edges against 0.539 on one edge:
  the edge costs +0.009 with the scan gap removed entirely, so the edge gap is sky or
  hardware rather than scan latency.
  Neither reaches 1.0, and it cannot: with dA and dB near 0.8 the two chains miss different cells,
  so phi is bounded well below 1 by detection probability alone before any sky effect enters.

  across rates (200,000 samples is both 80 ms at 2.5 MS/s and 160 ms at 1.25) and must not be
  used to bin this. x is the candidate's offset inside its own receiver's captured band, which
  is what the band edge clips; all-sky Doppler p99.9 is 279 kHz on its own.

  LNB bias moves the signal inside the ADC band. A biased port spends its guard before Doppler does,
  and it also populates the far bins on its own.
